- **add-incremental-loading**: Add incremental loading to a dlt filesystem pipeline — filter files by modification date, optionally filter records by a timestamp column, and switch to merge with a primary key to deduplicate updated records, so each run only reads new or modified files. Use after create-filesystem-pipeline produces a working replace-mode pipeline, or when the user wants to set up merge/dedup or move a pipeline from replace to incremental. For speed/memory tuning (faster reader, chunked streaming, parallel reads, glob narrowing) use optimize-filesystem-performance instead. ([dlt-hub/dlthub-ai-harness](https://github.com/dlt-hub/dlthub-ai-harness/tree/HEAD/workbench/filesystem-pipeline/skills/add-incremental-loading) · ★65 · 5 copies)
